- **zScoreMode** `[fn]` in [ChannelDataView](/docs/api/disp/channel-data-view)
- **zScoreMode** `[fn]` in [ChannelRhiView](/docs/api/disp/channel-rhi-view)
- **zScoreModeToggled** `[signal]` in [ChannelDataView](/docs/api/disp/channel-data-view)
- **zScoreRectify** `[fn]` in [InvCMNE](/docs/api/inv/inv-cmne) — Rectify and z-score each source over time (Eq.
- **zScores** `[var]` in [BadChannelsMaxwellResult](/docs/api/dsp/bad-channels-maxwell-result) — Per-channel z-scores of residuals.
- **zsize** `[var]` in [MNEVolGeom](/docs/api/mne/mne-vol-geom) — Voxel size in each direction (m; the file stores mm).
- **zsize** `[var]` in [MriVolData](/docs/api/mri/mri-vol-data) — Voxel spacing in Z direction (mm).
